    Chairman’s Statement
    董事長致辭

    Dear Shareholders and Investors:                                                   尊敬的各位股東及投資人朋友：


    It is with great pleasure and honor that herein I am exchanging thoughts           很榮幸能夠首次以上市公司董事會主席的身份
    with you as the Chairman of the Board of Directors of a listed company             與大家交流。感謝大家一直以來對我們的信
    for the first time. Thank you for your trust, support and assistance to us         任、支持和幫助。
    throughout our journey.


    The past year 2020 was a special year from a historical perspective. The           剛剛過去的2020 年是歷史視角下特殊的一年。
    outbreak of the Corona Virus Disease 2019 (“COVID-19”) pandemic gave rise          新型冠狀病毒（「新型冠狀病毒」）疫情的爆發使
    to tremendous changes in people’s lifestyles, and put the entire retail industry   人們的生活方式產生了巨大轉變，整個零售業
    under unprecedented challenges. Our retail stores and supply chains were           面臨前所未有的考驗。對於我們來說，線下門
    also correspondingly put to test. Nevertheless, through the efforts of all Pop     店和供應鏈也面臨着相應的挑戰。儘管如此，
    Mart staff, we still thrived to deliver excellent performance growth in such a     經過全體泡泡瑪特成員的努力，我們依舊在如
    severe situation. The total revenue for the year of 2020 amounted to RMB2.51       此嚴峻的情形中展現了優異的業績增長。2020
    billion, representing a year-on-year increase of 49.3%, and the adjusted net       年全年營收人民幣25.1億元，同比增長49.3%，
    profit reached RMB590 million. The challenges and opportunities presented          調整後淨利潤達到人民幣5.9 億元。疫情帶來
    by the pandemic prompted us to make sufficient progress in online                  的挑戰與機遇並存，使得我們在線上經濟化運
    commercial operations, supply chain flexibility, and member digitalization         營，供應鏈柔性化，以及會員數字化整合建設
    integration.                                                                       等方面也取得了充分的進展。


    2020 was also a year in which Pop Mart International Group Limited (the            2020 年也是對於泡泡瑪特國際集團有限公司
    “Company” or “Pop Mart”) marked its 10th anniversary, a significant                （「本公司」或「泡泡瑪特」）來說具有里程碑意義

    companionship and quality experience, and made a positive and solid                也為潮流文化產業做出了積極堅實的貢獻。目
    contribution to the pop culture industry. At present, we have deployed a           前，我們已佈局全球化業務版圖和多層次、多
    globalized business blueprint with a multi-level and multi-field business          領域的業務體系，在國內外開設近200家門店和
    system. We have reached nearly 200 stores and over 1,000 Roboshops around          1,000 多台機器人商店，吸引超過740 萬註冊會
    the world, attracting more than 7.4 million registered members to share the        員，共同分享對於潮流文化的認知。我們始終
    perception of pop culture. We pay close attention to the quality and effect        關注服務的品質和效果，將快樂在世界範圍傳

     Management Discussion and Analysis
     管理層討論與分析


     BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                    業務回顧
     As a pioneer and key promoter of pop toy culture in China, we sold over            作為中國潮流玩具文化的開拓者及主要推廣
     50 million pop toy products in 2020. We strategically focus on the further         者，2020 年，我們一共銷售超過 5,000 萬隻潮
     development of our pop toy business to consistently implement our long-            流玩具。我們戰略重點是進一步深耕潮流玩具
     term established strategy and development direction. During the year, we           業務，繼續貫徹公司的長期既定策略和發展方
     remained to regard artists development, IP operation, consumer access and          向。年內，我們仍以藝術家發掘、IP運營、消費
     pop toy culture promotion as our major business focus.                             者觸達及潮流玩具文化的推廣為主要業務重點。


     Artists Development and IP Operation                                               藝術家發掘和IP運營
     IP operation as well as creation and design are essential to our products,         IP運營和創作設計對於我們的產品至關重要，這
     which is the key driver for the development of Pop Mart. We continuously           也是推動泡泡瑪特發展的核心驅動力。我們不
     enrich our types of IP and launch products of diversified styles to meet the       斷豐富IP類型、輸出風格多樣化的產品，以滿足
     demands of different customers. During the year, the proportion of revenue         不同用戶群體的需求。年內，我們頭部IP收入佔
     from our top IPs was more balanced, and maintained a strong growth. As at          比更加均勻，並保持了強勁的增長，2020年8月
     the end of 2020, the sales of “one day of MOLLY” series, which were launched       上市的MOLLY的一天系列，截至2020 年底單銷
     in August 2020, had exceeded RMB100 million. We continuously signed                售額破人民幣1億元。我們持續簽約設計師並推
     contracts with designers to launch new IP product series. For example,             出新IP的產品系列，例如SKULLPANDA，一經推
     SKULLPANDA products were very popular among their fans immediately                 出就受到了粉絲的熱烈追捧，密林古堡系列首
     after their launch. On the first day of the launch of our Ancient Castle series,   發售出276,000 個，以及我們內部的設計師團隊
     276,000 products were sold. PDC (Pop Design Center), our in-house design           PDC (Pop Design Center)推出眾多受歡迎的IP，

Profitability                                                                       盈利能力
–   Costs of sales                                                                  － 銷售成本
    Our costs of sales increased by 55.0% from RMB593.1 million in 2019 to            我們的銷售成本由2019 年的人民幣593.1 百

         The non-IFRS adjusted net profit has not been calculated in accordance         非國際財務報告準則經調整純利未按照國
         with the IFRS, thus it is deemed as non-IFRS financial indicator. The          際財務報告準則計算，視為非國際財務報
         non-IFRS adjusted net profit refers to the net profit after excluding          告準則的財務指標，其定義為除上市費
         listing expenses, share-based payment, expenses in relation to the             用、以股份為基礎的付款報酬、重新指定
         redesignation of ordinary shares as preference shares and changes in           普通股為優先股的有相關開支、可轉換可
         fair values of convertible redeemable preference shares, while the non-        贖回優先股的公平值變動後純利，而非國
         IFRS adjusted net profit margin refers to the non-IFRS adjusted net            際財務報告準則經調整純利率的定義為非
         profit divided by revenue. We are of the view that such information is         國際財務報告準則經調整純利除以收益。
         useful for investors to compare the results of the Group, provided that        我們認為，這些信息對於投資者在不影響
         the results of the continuing operation or cash flows of the Group are         本集團持續經營業績或現金流的情況下比
         not being affected, and enable investors to take into consideration of         較本集團的業績是有用的，並允許投資者
         the indicators used by the management when assessing the results               考慮管理層在評估本集團業績時使用的指
         of the Group. Investors shall not treat non-IFRS financial indicator as        標。投資者不應認為非國際財務報告準則
         an alternative or better version of the results of the Group prepared in       的財務指標替代或優於本集團的國際財務
         accordance with IFRS. In addition, not all companies will adopt the same       報告準則的業績。此外，可能不是所有公
         way in calculating such non-IFRS financial indicators. Hence, similar          司會以相同的方式計算該項非國際財務報
         measurements made by other companies may not be comparable.                    告準則的財務指標，因此未必可與其他公
                                                                                        司採用的相若計量比較。

     Reasons for Adoption of the Contractual Arrangements                             採納合約安排的理由
     The Relevant Businesses of Paqu Huyu are subject to foreign investment           葩趣互娛的相關業務受相關中國法律法規之外
     restrictions in accordance with relevant PRC laws and regulations. Paqu          商投資限制規限。葩趣互娛目前持有ICP許可證
     Huyu currently holds an ICP License and an ICB License, which are required       及 I C B 許可證，均為營運相關業務所必需的。
     for the operation of the Relevant Businesses. After consultation with our        經諮詢我們的中國法律顧問後，我們認為本集
     PRC Legal Advisor, we determined that it was not viable for our Group to         團直接持有葩趣互娛的任何股權並不可行。相
     directly hold any equity ownership in Paqu Huyu. Instead, we decided that,       反，我們認為，根據受外商投資限制規限的中
     in line with common practice in industries in the PRC subject to foreign         國行業慣例，透過北京泡泡瑪特（本公司於中國
     investment restrictions, we would gain effective control over, and receive all   成立的全資附屬公司，作為一方）與葩趣互娛及
     the economic benefits generated by the business currently operated by Paqu       相關股東（作為另一方）之間的合約安排，我們
     Huyu through the Contractual Arrangements between Beijing Pop Mart, a            將獲得對葩趣互娛的實際控制權，並取得葩趣
     wholly-owned subsidiary of our Company established in the PRC, on the one        互娛目前經營的業務所產生的所有經濟利益。
     hand, and Paqu Huyu and the Relevant Shareholders, on the other hand.

     STAFF, EMOLUMENT POLICY AND DIRECTORS ’                                           員工、薪酬政策及董事酬金
     REMUNERATION
     The Company offers competitive remuneration packages to the Directors.            本公司向董事提供具競爭力的薪酬待遇。薪酬
     The packages were set by benchmarking with companies in similar industries,       待遇乃通過與行業、市場規模、工作量及複雜
     companies with similar size in the market, volume and complexity of work.         程度相近的公司進行基準測試釐定。其他酬金
     Other emoluments are determined by the Board with reference to the                乃由董事會參考董事的職責、責任及表現以及
     Directors’ duties, responsibilities and performance and the results of the        本集團的業績釐定。
     Group.


     The remuneration payable to our employees includes salaries, bonuses and          應付僱員酬金包括薪金、花紅及僱員福利計劃
     employee benefit plans contributions. The salaries of our employees are           供款。僱員薪金通常按僱員各自的職位、資

     experience and performance. The bonuses are calculated based on our               位、服務年期及僱員表現計算。為吸引並挽留
     employees’ respective position, duration of services and performance of           僱員，我們每年評估提供予僱員的薪酬方案，
     our employees. In order to attract and retain our employees, we assess            以確定是否應對基本薪金及花紅作出任何調整。
     the remuneration package offered to our employees on an annual basis to
     determine whether any adjustment to the basic salaries and bonus should be
     made.

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                             風險管理及內部控制
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management and             董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制系統負有
internal control systems and reviewing their effectiveness. Such systems          責任，並負責檢討其成效。該等系統旨在管理
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve       而非消除未能達成業務目標的風險，且僅能就
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not absolute             並無重大失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對保
assurance against material misstatement or loss.                                  證。


The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and determining the       董事會全面負責評估及釐定本公司達成戰略目
nature and extent of the risks it is willing to take in achieving the Company’s   標時所願意承擔的風險性質及程度，並設立及
strategic objectives, and establishing and maintaining appropriate and            維護適當而有效的風險管理及內部控制系統。
effective risk management and internal control systems.

     The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the management                     審核委員會協助董事會領導管理層並監督風險
     and overseeing the design, implementation and monitoring of the risk                管理及內部控制系統的設計、實施及監控。部
     management and internal control systems. Heads of departments manage                門主管透過識別、評估及降低已識別風險進行
     risks through identification, evaluation and mitigation of risk identified.         風險管理。


     Risk management mainly includes four major areas: risks identification, risks       風險管理主要包括四個主要領域：風險識別、
     assessment, risks response and risk monitoring and reporting.                       風險評估、風險應對以及風險監控及報告。


     Risk identification: each business and functional department and its                風險識別：各業務及職能部門以及其附屬公司
     subsidiaries conducts, once in a year, an identification of potential internal      每年進行一次識別其各自營運流程中潛在的內
     and external risks in its respective operation processes. During risks              部及外部風險。於識別風險期間，主要參考風
     identification, references are mainly made to the impact the risks have on the      險對本公司目標的影響以及過去一年業務活動
     Company’s objectives, and major problems or risk incidents in the business          中的重大問題或風險事件。
     activities for the past year.


     Risk assessment: according to the risks assessment standards, each business         風險評估：根據風險評估標準，各業務及職能
     and functional department and its subsidiaries reviews the risks identified,        部門以及其附屬公司對已識別風險進行審查，
     and assesses the possibilities of occurrence and the extents of impacts in          並評估發生的可能性及影響程度，以篩選出重
     order to screen out the significant risks. The Group adopts a combination of        大風險。本集團採納自下而上及自上而下的風
     bottom-up and top-down risk assessment procedures to fully identify all of          險評估程序，以全面識別本集團所有重大風
     the Group’s significant risks, which are then given rankings. Significant risks     險，其後對其進行排名。隨後，向相應的管理
     are then reported to the appropriate management level, Audit Committee              層、審核委員會及董事會報告重大風險。進行
     and the Board. A final list of significant risks is confirmed after thorough        充分溝通及討論後，最終確認重大風險清單。
     communication and discussion.


     Risks response: the responsible department of the identified risks formulates       風險應對：經考慮本集團的風險承受能力，已
     a risk response plan by properly applying methods such as risk avoidance, risk      識別風險的負責部門通過適當運用諸如規避風
     reduction, risk sharing and risk acceptance, with consideration of the Group’s      險、降低風險、分擔風險及接受風險等方法制
     level of risk tolerance. This allows the Group to properly allocate resources for   定風險應對計劃。此舉令本集團能適當分配資
     risk mitigation or improvements on risk response measures, with an aim to           源以緩解風險或改善風險應對措施，藉以將本
     reduce the overall risk of the Group to an acceptable level.                        集團的整體風險降至可接受水平。


     Risk monitoring and reporting: risk monitoring and reporting are carried            風險監控及報告：風險監控及報告乃通過整合
     out by integrating the use of risk warning indicators, internal auditing and        使用風險警告指標、內部審核及定期匯總風險
     periodic summarized risk reports.                                                   報告進行。

II. ESG MANAGEMENT SYSTEM                                                       二、ESG管理體系
  ESG Governance Structure and Management Concept                                   ESG管治架構及管理理念
  As an enterprise strives to promote pop toy culture, the Company has a            作為一家推廣潮玩文化的公司，本公司以
  vision of “become a leading global pop culture entertainment company”,           「成為全球領先的潮流文化娛樂公司」作為
  and adhere to create a comprehensive operating platform covering the              公司願景，致力於打造覆蓋潮流玩具全產
  entire industry chain of pop toys. We proactively focus on the demands            業鏈的綜合運營平台。我們積極關注各利
  of stakeholders, and perform our corporate social responsibilities while          益相關方訴求，在保障股東及投資者利益
  protecting the interests of our shareholders and investors.                       的同時履行企業社會責任。


  To implement the development philosophy of the Company and                        為貫徹公司發展理念，推進公司ESG管理工
  facilitate the implementation of ESG management of the Company,                   作落實，本公司依託當前管理組織架構，
  based on the current management organizational structure, the                     建立了由董事會領導、總裁辦監管、多個
  Company has established the ESG governance structure, which is led by             職能部門共同參與的ESG管治架構。董事會
  the board of directors (the “Board”), supervised by president office and          定期審閱ESG報告，研究和討論ESG事宜，
  jointly participated by several functional departments. The Board will            指導和監督管理層開展 E S G 相關工作，總
  review the ESG report, investigate and discuss ESG issues, and provide            裁辦負責協助董事會監管ESG策略和管理事
  guidance and supervision to the management in conducting ESG-related              宜的落實情況，各業務條線總監級負責人
  works on a regular basis. The president office will be responsible for            負責識別和把控各自負責領域的ESG相關風
  assisting the Board in monitoring the implementation of ESG strategies            險，相關職能部門負責實施ESG具體工作，
  and management. Responsible personnel (director level) from different             保障ESG策略和相關工作的全面落實。
  business units will be responsible for identifying and controlling
  ESG-related risk exposures in their respective responsible fields. Relevant
  functional departments will be responsible for the implementation of
  specific ESG works, as well as the full implementation of ESG strategies
  and relevant works.


  During the Reporting Period, the Company actively identified and                  報告期內，本公司主動識別並嚴格遵守運
  strictly complied with relevant ESG laws and regulations in countries             營所在國家和地區的ESG相關法律法規，將
  and regions where it operates. The Company incorporated its ESG                   公司的ESG管理理念融入到日常經營與管理

  Emission Management                                                          排放物管理

  the business operation of the Company mainly include greenhouse              產生的排放物主要包括因使用用電設備、

  equipments, as well as wastes such as daily office garbage.                  辦公垃圾等廢棄物。


  We adopt various measures to conduct carbon emission management,             我們採用多種措施開展碳排放管理，制定
  formulate policies on energy saving, emission reduction and low carbon       節能減排、低碳環保等方針，倡導員工低
  environmental protection, promote low-carbon travel for our employees.       碳出行。在產品出庫和運輸時，我們倡導
  When delivering and transporting our products, we advocate the low           低碳理念，通過使用倉庫提貨預報的模式
  carbon concept. By adopting the advanced notification mode for stock         合理安排車輛提送貨，提高車輛裝載率，
  collection, we will arrange trucks for goods pick-up and delivery in a       避免空駛、大車裝少貨的情況發生，有效
  reasonable manner, and increase the truck loading rate so as to avoid        降低貨物運輸碳排放。我們亦持續採取多
  empty truck and large truck transported with a few goods, effectively        種節能措施，加強辦公區及門店的用電管

Electricity Consumption Management                                             用電管理
The Company constantly adheres to the concept of “electricity                  本公司在業務策略及日常運營中持續貫徹
conservation” in its business strategies and daily operation, and adopts      「節約用電」的理念，採取以下措施以提高
the following measures to enhance the energy efficiency of the                 公司的能效：
Company:


•   Office temperature will be set at 26℃ in summer and 20℃ in winter          •   夏季將辦公室空調溫度設定為 26 攝氏
    so as to reduce electricity consumption and avoid waste of energy;             度，冬季設定為20 攝氏度，節約用
                                                                                   電，杜絕浪費；


•   Employees are encouraged to turn off unnecessary lightings,                •   鼓勵員工於午餐時段、外出時及下班
    heaters, personal computers, monitors and other devices during                 後關掉不必要的電燈、電暖、個人電
    lunch hour, out for work and off duty. Employees are reminded to               腦及顯示幕等設備，提醒員工做到人
    turn off lightings while leaving office;                                       走燈滅；


•   The Company will enhance electricity consumption management in             •   加強門店用電管理，除正常營業時間
    stores. Except for normal opening hours, only emergence lighting               外，其他時間段均只保留消防應急照
    will be turned on in other time slot;                                          明；


•   The Company will strengthen lighting management in warehouses,             •   加強庫區照明管理，提高自然光線利
    and increase natural light utilization rate. In cloudy days or when            用率，在陰天或天色變暗時鼓勵交錯

         Water Management                                                             用水管理
         To improve water efficiency, we have adopted the following measures          為提高用水效益，我們已採取以下措施減
         to reduce water consumption:                                                 少水資源消耗：

             we post “Water-saving” slogans in office area;                               強員工的水資源節約意識；


         •   Water-saving taps are installed so as to control water velocity;         •   使用節水龍頭，控制水龍頭的水流速
                                                                                          度；


         •   We have increased the inspection and daily maintenance of water          •   加強巡查及對水龍頭、水管、蓄水池
             taps, pipelines, water tanks and other facilities so as to prevent           等設施的日常維護管理，嚴防滴、
             water drip, water leakage, water spray and other waste of water.             漏、冒等水資源浪費的情況發生。


         Packaging Materials Management                                               包裝材料管理
         The packaging materials of our products primarily include color coated       本公司產品的包裝材料主要包括銅版紙彩

         box for logistics etc. In 2020, we replaced the PVC (polyvinyl chloride)     袋、物流瓦楞紙箱等。2020 年，我們將產
         plastic counterweight in our product package with biodegradable pure         品包裝內的PVC（聚氯乙烯）塑料配重幣更換
         diatomaceous granule and spunlace non-woven fabric counterweight             為由可降解硅藻純顆粒和水刺無紡布製成
         pack. We have also stopped using normal aluminum foil bag, and               的配重包，並棄用普通鋁塑袋，選用環保
         selected to use biodegradable aluminum foil bag, which is more               性較好的可生物降解鋁塑袋，同時盡可能
         environmental-friendly. At the same time, we reduce the usage of             減少包裝材料使用量，將包裝的體積和重
         packing materials as much as possible, and control the volume and            量控制在最低限，力求在運營過程中盡量
         weight of packaging materials at the lowest level, aiming to minimize        減少包裝材料對環境的影響。
         the impacts of packaging materials on environment during our
         operation.

        Reduction Project issued by the Ministry of Ecology and Environment of                     項目中國區域電網基準線排放因子》及政府
        the People’s Republic of China, and the 2006 IPCC Guidelines for National                  間氣候變化專門委員會 (IPCC) 刊發的《IPCC
        Greenhouse Gas Inventories (2019 Revision) issued by the Intergovernmental                 2006年國家溫室氣體清單指南2019修訂版》
        Panel on Climate Change (IPCC).                                                            進行核算。

and control work.

Key Audit Matter                How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
關鍵審計事項                          我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項

                                •   We tested the aging profile of inventories items on a
                                    sample basis by checking to relevant documents including
                                    goods receipt notes;
                                •   通過與相關單據（包括收貨單）核對，我們在抽樣的基礎
                                    上對存貨的庫齡結構進行了測試；


                                •   We compared the carrying amount of the inventories, on
                                    a sample basis, to their net realisable value through review
                                    of sales subsequent to the year end;
                                •   我們通過對年末以後的銷售情況進行審查，在抽樣的基
                                    礎上將存貨的賬面價值與可變現淨值進行了比較；


                                •   We assessed the reasonableness of methods and
                                    assumptions applied to the provisions by challenging
                                    management’s future sales projection, estimated future
                                    selling prices and selling expenses, with reference to
                                    industry trend and approved budget developed based on
                                    historical data;
                                •   我們通過質疑管理層的未來銷售預測、未來銷售價格和
                                    銷售費用的估計，並參考行業趨勢和根據歷史資料制定
                                    的經審批預算，評估撥備方法和假設的合理性；


                                •   We checked the mathematical accuracy of the calculation
                                    of the provision for inventories;
                                •   我們檢查了存貨減值準備計算的準確性；


                                •   We observed the physical condition of inventories during
                                    stocktake to identify if any inventories were damaged,
                                    or obsolete and inquired management if appropriate
                                    inventories provision has been made.
                                •   我們在存貨盤點期間查看了存貨的物理狀況，以確定存
                                    貨是否出現損壞或報廢，並詢問管理層是否已作出適當
                                    的存貨減值準備。


                                We found the assumptions adopted and judgment applied by
                                management in the determination of impairment provision for
                                inventories were supported by the evidence we gathered.
                                我們認為，管理層在確定存貨減值準備時採用的假設和運用
                                的判斷均有據可依。

communicated in our report because the adverse consequences of doing so         過產生的公眾利益，我們決定不應在報告中溝

1 GENERAL INFORMATION                                                          1 一般資料
  Pop Mart International Group Limited (the “Company”) was incorporated          泡 泡 瑪 特 國 際 集 團 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）
  in the Cayman Islands on 9 May 2019 as an exempted company with                於2019 年5 月9 日根據開曼群島公司法第22
  limited liability under the Companies Law (Cap. 22, Law 3 of 1961 as           章（1961 年第 3 號法例，經綜合及修訂）在
  consolidated and revised) of the Cayman Islands. The address of the            開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。本

2.2 Changes in accounting policies                                            2.2 會計政策的變動
   The Group has early adopted Amendment to IFRS 16 – COVID-19-Related          本集團已自2020 年1 月1 日起追溯提早採納
   Rent Concessions retrospectively from 1 January 2020. The amendment          國際財務報告準則第16 號修訂 － 與新型冠
   provides an optional practical expedient allowing lessees to elect not       狀病毒疫情相關的租金優惠。該修訂提供
   to assess whether a rent concession related to COVID-19 is a lease           一項可選擇的實際權宜方法，容許承租人
   modification. Lessees adopting this election may account for qualifying      選擇不評估與新型冠狀病毒疫情相關的租
   rent concessions in the same way as they would if they were not lease        金優惠是否屬租賃修訂。採納這選項的承
   modifications. The practical expedient only applies to rent concessions      租人可按租金優惠並非租賃修訂的相同方
   occurring as a direct consequence of the COVID-19 pandemic and only          式入賬合資格租金優惠。實際權宜方法僅
   if all of the following conditions are met:                                  適用於新型冠狀病毒疫情直接導致且符合
                                                                                以下所有條件的租金優惠：


   •    the change in lease payments results in revised consideration           •   租賃付款變動導致租賃的經修訂代價
        for the lease that is substantially the same as, or less than, the          與緊接變動前的租賃代價大致相同或
        consideration for the lease immediately preceding the change;               較其為低；


   •    any reduction in lease payments affects only payments originally        •   租賃付款減少僅會影響於2021年6月30
        due on or before 30 June 2021; and                                          日或之前到期的付款；及


   •    there is no substantive change to other terms and conditions of the     •   租賃的其他條款和條件並無實質性變
        lease.                                                                      化。


   The Group has applied the practical expedient to all qualifying COVID-       本集團就所有合資格與新型冠狀病毒疫情
   19-related rent concessions. Rent concessions totaling approximately         相關的租金優惠採用實際權宜方法。合共
   RMB7,229,000 have been accounted for as negative variable lease              約人民幣7,229,000 元的租金優惠已入賬為
   payments and recognised in distribution and selling expenses and             負可變租賃付款，並於綜合損益及其他全
   general and administrative expenses in the consolidated statement of         面收益表確認為分銷及銷售開支以及一般

      2.3 Summary of significant accounting policies                                   2.3 綜合入賬原則
         (i) Subsidiaries                                                                (i) 附屬公司
             Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured entities) over            附屬公司為本集團控制的所有實體（包
             which the Group has control. The Group controls an entity when                括 結 構 實 體 ）。 倘 本 集 團 須 承 擔 或 享
             the Group is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from              有參與實體所獲可變回報的風險及權
             its involvement with the entity and has the ability to affect those           利，且有能力透過其指導實體活動之
             returns through its power to direct the activities of the entity.             權力影響該等回報時，則本集團對該
             Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on which control            實體擁有控制權。附屬公司自控制權
             is transferred to the Group. They are deconsolidated from the date            轉入本集團之日起全面綜合入賬，自
             that control ceases.                                                          停止控制之日起取消綜合入賬。


             Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised gains on                  公司間交易、集團公司間交易的結餘
             transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealised               及未變現收益予以對銷。除非交易提
             losses are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence           供所轉讓資產的減值證據，否則未變
             of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                現虧損亦將予以對銷。附屬公司的會
             of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                   計政策已作出必要之更改，以確保與
             consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                           本集團所採納的政策一致。


             Non-controlling interests in the results and equity of subsidiaries           於附屬公司業績及股權的非控股權益
             are shown separately in the consolidated statement of profit of loss          分別於綜合損益及其他全面收益表、
             and other comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity                權益變動表及資產負債表單獨列示。
             and balance sheet respectively.


             Subsidiary controlled through Contractual Arrangements                        通過合約安排控制的附屬公司
             There is an entity controlled by the Group under Contractual                  本集團根據合約安排控制一家實體。
             Arrangements. The Group does not have legal ownership in                      本集團對結構實體的股權並無合法所
             equity of the structured entity. Nevertheless, under Contractual              有權。儘管如此，根據與結構實體的
             Arrangement entered into with the Registered Shareholders of                  登記股東訂立的合約安排，本公司及
             the structured entity, the Company and its other legally owned                其他合法擁有的附屬公司透過控制
             subsidiaries controlled the structured entity by way of controlling           投票權、規管其財務及經營政策、委
             the voting rights, governing its financial and operating policies,            任或罷免其控制權力部門的大部分成
             appointing or removing the majority of members of its controlling             員，以及於有關部門的會議上行使過
             authorities, and casting the majority of votes at meetings of                 半數投票權，從而控制結構實體。
             such authorities. Accordingly, the Group has rights to exercise               因此，本集團有權對結構實體行使權
             power over the structured entity, receives variable returns from              力、參與結構實體獲得可變回報，因
             involvement in the structured entity. As a result, it is presented as a       此作為本集團的控制結構實體呈列。
             controlled structured entity of the Group.

          (iv) Equity method                                                              (iv) 權益法
              Under the equity method of accounting, the investments are                    根據權益會計法，投資初步按成本確
              initially recognised at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the         認，其後進行調整以確認本集團在損
              Group’s share of the post-acquisition profits or losses of the investee       益中分佔被投資方的收購後損益，以
              in profit or loss, and the Group’s share of movements in other                及本集團在其他全面收益中分佔被投
              comprehensive income of the investee in other comprehensive                   資方的其他全面收益變動。已收或應
              income. Dividends received or receivable from joint ventures are              收合營企業股息確認為投資賬面值的
              recognised as a reduction in the carrying amount of the investment.           減少。



              Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-accounted                      如本集團分佔以權益入賬投資虧損等
              investment equals or exceeds its interest in the entity, including            於或超過其於實體中的權益（包括任何
              any other unsecured long-term receivables, the Group does not                 其他無抵押長期應收款項），本集團不

   The assets’ residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and adjusted if      資產的剩餘價值及可使用年期於各結算日
   appropriate, at each balance sheet date.                                        均會進行檢討，並在適當情況下作出調整。


   An asset’s carrying amount is written down immediately to its                   倘資產賬面值高於其估計可收回金額，則
   recoverable amount if the asset’s carrying amount is greater than its           會立即將該資產的賬面值撇減至其可收回
   estimated recoverable amount.                                                   金額。


   Gains and losses on disposals are determined by comparing the                   出售的收益及虧損透過將所得款項與賬面
   proceeds with the carrying amount and are recognised within “Other              值比較而釐定，並於綜合損益及其他全面
   gains/(losses)-net” in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and         收 益 表「 其 他 收 益 ╱（ 虧 損 ）－ 淨 額 」內 確
   other comprehensive income.                                                     認。

          which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable amount. The           其可收回金額之部分予以確認。可收回金

          recognised amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis           產和結算負債時，金融資產與負債可互相
          or realise the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The legally       抵銷，並在資產負債表呈報其淨額。合法

      2.13 Inventories                                                                 2.13 存貨
          Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost      存貨以成本及可變現淨值兩者中的較低者
          is determined using the weighted average method. Costs of purchased             列賬。成本使用加權平均法釐定。購買存
          inventory are determined after deducting rebates and discounts. It              貨的成本乃於扣除回扣及折扣後釐定，不
          excludes borrowing costs. Net realisable value is the estimated selling         包括借款成本。可變現淨值為在正常經營
          price in the ordinary course of business less the estimated costs of            過程中的估計售價減去估計的完工成本和
          completion and the estimated costs necessary to make the sale.                  估計的銷售所需成本。

          normal operating cycle of the business if longer), they are classified as       仍在正常業務經營週期中，則可更長時間）
          current assets. If not, they are presented as non-current assets.               收回，其被分類為流動資產，否則分類為

          Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount of consideration       貿易應收款項初步按有關無條件代價金額
          that is unconditional unless they contain significant financing                 確認，但當其包含重大融資部分時，則按

      2.20 Current and deferred income tax                                                 2.20 即期及遞延所得稅
         The income tax expense or credit for the period is the tax payable on               期內所得稅開支或抵免指就本期間應課稅
         the current period’s taxable income, based on the applicable income tax             收入按各司法權區適用所得稅稅率應付的
         rate for each jurisdiction, adjusted by changes in deferred tax assets and          稅項，並就暫時性差額及未動用稅項虧損
         liabilities attributable to temporary differences and to unused tax losses.         應佔遞延稅項資產及負債變動作出調整。


         (a) Current income tax                                                              (a) 即期所得稅
              The current income tax charge is calculated on the basis of the                  即期所得稅支出根據本集團及其附屬
              tax laws enacted or substantively enacted at the balance sheet                   公司業務經營及產生應課稅收入的所
              date in the countries where the Group and its subsidiaries operate               在國家於結算日已頒佈或實質上已頒
              and generate taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                   佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就適用稅
              positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in which               務法例詮釋所規限的情況定期評估報
              applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation. It establishes           稅狀況，並在適用情況下根據預期須
              provisions where appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to                 向稅務機關支付的稅款作出撥備。
              be paid to the tax authorities.

2.24 Leases                                                                       2.24 租賃
    Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding                租賃乃於租賃資產可供集團使用之日確認
    liability at the date at which the leased asset is available for use by the      為使用權資產及相關負債。
    Group.


    Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group             合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組成部分。本
    allocates the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease           集團按照租賃及非租賃組成部分相應的獨
    components based on their relative stand-alone prices. However, for              立價格，將合約代價分配至租賃及非租賃
    leases of real estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has elected not        組成部分。然而，就本集團作為承租人的
    to separate lease and non-lease components and instead accounts for              房地產租賃而言，其選擇將租賃及非租賃
    these as a single lease component.                                               組成部分入賬為單一租賃組成部分，並無
                                                                                     將兩者區分。


    Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide             租賃條款乃單獨磋商達致，當中包含各種
    range of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not             不同的條款及條件。租賃協議並不強加任
    impose any covenants other than the security interests in the leased             何 契 諾（ 於 出 租 人 持 有 的 租 賃 資 產 中 之 抵
    assets that are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as             押權益除外）。租賃資產不得用作借款的抵
    security for borrowing purposes.                                                 押。


    Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a          租賃產生的資產及負債初步以現值基準計
    present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of the      量。租賃負債包括下列租賃付款的淨現值：
    following lease payments:

        lease incentives receivable                                                      減去任何應收租賃優惠


    •   variable lease payment that are based on an index or a rate, initially       •   基於指數或利率的可變租賃付款，採
        measured using the index or rate as at the commencement date                     用於開始日期的指數或利率初步計量


    •   amounts expected to be payable by the Group under residual value             •   本集團於剩餘價值擔保下預計應付的
        guarantees                                                                       金額


    •   the exercise price of a purchase option if the Group is reasonably           •   倘本集團合理確定行使購買選擇權，
        certain to exercise that option, and                                             則為該選擇權的行使價，及


    •   payments of penalties for terminating the lease, if the lease term           •   倘租期反映本集團行使該選擇權，則
        reflects the Group exercising that option.                                       為終止租賃的罰款付款。

               adjusted for credit risk for leases held by the Group, which does not           賃的信貸風險（最近並無第三方融資）

               and security.                                                                   家、貨幣及抵押。

          payment profile to the lease, then the group entities use that rate as a         付款情況與租賃相似，則集團實體將以該
          starting point to determine the incremental borrowing rate.                      利率為起點釐定增量借款利率。

          payments based on an index or rate, which are not included in the lease          租賃付款額，而有關指數或利率在生效前
          liability until they take effect. When adjustments to lease payments             不會計入租賃負債。當根據指數或利率對
          based on an index or rate take effect, the lease liability is reassessed and     租賃付款作出的調整生效時，租賃負債會
          adjusted against the right-of-use asset.                                         根據使用權資產進行重新評估及調整。


          Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance cost. The             租賃付款於本金及財務成本之間作出分
          finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period so as to         配。財務成本在租賃期間於損益扣除，藉
          produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of         以令各期間的負債餘額的期間利率一致。
          the liability for each period.

      3 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                           3 財務風險管理
      3.1 Financial risk factors                                                            3.1 財務風險因素
          The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks, primarily            本集團的活動使其面臨各種財務風險，主
          the credit risk and liquidity risk. The Group’s overall risk management                要是信貸風險及流動資金風險。本集團的
          programme focuses on the unpredictability of financial markets and                     整體風險管理計劃著重於金融市場的不可
          seeks to minimise potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial                   預測性，力求將對本集團財務業績的潛在

            The Group applies the IFRS 9 simplified approach to measure                 本集團採用國際財務報告準則第9
            expected credit losses which uses a lifetime expected loss                  號的簡化方法計量預期信貸虧損，
            allowance for all account receivables. To measure the expected              該方法對所有應收賬款使用全期預

            are adjusted to reflect current and forward-looking information             作出調整，以反映有關影響客戶結

            customers to settle the receivables.                                        當前及前瞻性資料。

    (c) Liquidity risk                                                              (c) 流動資金風險
        The Group aims to maintain sufficient cash and cash equivalents.               本集團旨在維持足夠的現金及現金等
        Due to the dynamic nature of the underlying business, the Group’s              價物。由於相關業務的動態性質，本
        finance department maintains flexibility in funding by maintaining             集團的財務部透過維持足夠的現金及
        adequate cash and cash equivalents.                                            現金等價物來維持資金的靈活性。


        Cash flow forecasting is performed by the finance department of                現金流量預測由本集團財務部進行。
        the Group. The finance department of the Group monitors rolling                本集團財務部監控本集團流動資金需
        forecasts of the Group’s liquidity requirements to ensure it has               求的滾動預測，以確保其有足夠的現
        sufficient cash to meet operational needs as well as the liabilities to        金來滿足運營需求以及對其他方的負
        other parties.                                                                 債。

3 FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT (continued)                                         3 財務風險管理（續）
3.2 Capital management                                                          3.2 資本管理
   The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the              本集團管理資本之目的為維持其持續經營
   Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide             的能力，以便為本集團擁有人提供回報，
   returns for owners of the Group and to maintain an optimal capital             並維持最佳的資本架構以提高擁有人的長
   structure to enhance owners’ value in the long term.                           期價值。


   The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio       本集團按照資本負債率監控資本。該比率
   is calculated as net debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated     按債務淨額除以總資金計算。債務淨額按
   as total borrowings less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is           借款總額減去現金及現金等價物計算。總
   calculated as ‘equity’ as shown in the balance sheet plus net debt. As at      資金按資產負債表所示「權益」加債務淨額
   31 December 2020 and 31 December 2019, the Group has no net debt.              計算。於2020年12月31日及2019年12月31
                                                                                  日，本集團並無債務淨額。

   recognised and measured at fair value in the financial statements.             估計。為得出釐定公平值所用輸入數據的

   into the three levels prescribed under the accounting standards. An            如下表所示。
   explanation of each level follows underneath the table.

             reporting period. The quoted market price used for financial                   市場報價。本集團持有的金融資產
             assets held by the Group is the current bid price. These                       使用的市場報價為當前買入價。該
             instruments are included in level 1.                                           等工具列入層級1中。

             traded in an active market (for example, over-the-counter                      融工具（如場外衍生工具）之公平
             derivatives) is determined using valuation techniques which                    值乃採用估值技術釐定，該等估值
             maximise the use of observable market data and rely as                         技術盡量使用可觀察市場數據而極
             little as possible on entity-specific estimates. If all significant            少依賴於實體的特定估計。倘計算
             inputs required to fair value an instrument are observable, the                工具之公平值所需的全部重大輸入
             instrument is included in level 2.                                             數據均為可觀察數據，則該工具列
                                                                                            入層級2中。

4 CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                              4 關鍵會計估計及判斷
  JUDGMENTS
    Estimates and judgments are continually evaluated and are based on             我們根據過往經驗及其他因素（包括在有關
    historical experience and other factors, including expectations of future      情況下對未來事件作出相信屬合理之預期）
    events that are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.             持續評估估計及判斷。


    The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the future.               本集團就未來作出有關估計及假設。顧名
    The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal           思義，該等會計估計甚少與有關實際結果
    the related actual results. The estimates and assumptions that have a          相同。有重大風險可能導致於下個財政年
    significant risk of causing a material adjustment to the carrying amounts      度內須對資產及負債之賬面值作出重大調
    of assets and liabilities within the next financial year are discussed         整之估計及假設討論如下。
    below.

      4 CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                              4 關鍵會計估計及判斷（續）
        JUDGMENTS (continued)
      (a) Impairment provision for inventories                                         (a) 存貨減值撥備
          The estimation of impairment provision for inventories involves                存貨的減值撥備估計涉及管理層的重大判
          significant management judgment based on the consideration of                  斷，該等判斷需考慮相關存貨的賬齡結
          key factors such as aging profile, future sales projection, estimated          構、未來銷售預測、預估未來售價及銷售
          future selling prices and selling expenses of the respective inventories.      開支等關鍵因素。管理層於每個結算日重
          Management reassesses these estimations of impairment provision for            新評估存貨減值撥備的該等預測。
          inventories at each balance sheet date.


      (b) Useful lives of Licensed IPs and IP rights                                   (b) 授權IP及IP產權的可使用年期
          The Group has Licensed IPs and IP rights that are primarily used to            本集團擁有授權IP及IP產權，主要用於設計
          design and sell pop toys. The Group estimates the useful life of these         及銷售潮流玩具。根據合約及預期的經濟
          Licensed IPs and IP rights to be 2-10 years based on the contract and          利益，本集團估計該等授權IP及IP產權的可
          expected economic benefit. However, the actual useful life may be              使用年期為兩至十年。然而，實際的可使
          shorter or longer than management’s estimate, depending on the                 用年期可能短於或長於管理層的估計，其
          market competition. Periodic review could result in a change in useful         取決於市場競爭情況。定期檢討可能導致
          lives and therefore amortisation expense in future periods.                    可使用年期發生變動，從而導致未來期間
                                                                                         的攤銷開支變動。

   EQUITY METHOD (continued)
  (a)   Although the Group holds 52% equity interests of Nanjing Pop Mart, in                 (a)   儘管本集團持有南京泡泡瑪特的52%股權，

36 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                                    36 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability, directly     若一方有能力直接或間接控制另一方，或
   or indirectly, control the other party or exercise significant influence        在作出財務及經營決策時對另一方發揮重
   over the other party in making financial and operation decisions. Parties       大影響，即視為有關聯。倘所涉各方受共
   are also considered to be related if they are subject to common control.        同控制，則亦視為有關聯。泡泡瑪特的主
   Members of key management and their close family member of Popmart              要管理層成員及其近親成員亦被視為關聯
   are also considered as related parties.                                         方。

         dated 19 August 2020 in relation to a claim brought by Golden Eagle            份日期為 2020 年 8 月 19 日的法院傳票，內

         Intermediate People’s Court against Beijing Pop Mart, alleging, among          特於江蘇省南京市中級人民法院向北京泡
         others, that Beijing Pop Mart had breached an investment cooperation           泡瑪特提起的申索，指稱（其中包括）北京
         agreement dated 29 April 2014 which was entered into among Golden              泡泡瑪特違反日期為2014年4月29日由金鷹
         Eagle International, Mr. Wang Ning and other then shareholders of              國際、王寧先生及北京泡泡瑪特當時的其
         Beijing Pop Mart. Pursuant to the claim, Golden Eagle International            他股東訂立的投資合作協議。根據申索，
         claimed that (i) Beijing Pop Mart should cease to operate the claimed          金鷹國際要求 (i) 北京泡泡瑪特應停止於若
         stores in certain exclusive areas; and (ii) Beijing Pop Mart should return     干專屬地區經營申索店舖；及(ii)北京泡泡
         an amount of approximately RMB117.2 million to Nanjing Pop Mart,               瑪特應向南京泡泡瑪特返還為數約人民幣
         which represents the alleged gross profits obtained by Beijing Pop Mart        117.2 百萬元的款項，即北京泡泡瑪特於截

         in the exclusive areas.                                                        營申索店舖獲得的所指稱毛利。


         No provision in relation to this claim has been recognised in the              概無就該項申索於綜合財務報表確認任何
         consolidated financial statements, as the PRC legal adviser of the             撥備，原因是本公司的中國法律顧問告
         Company advised that the claim brought by Golden Eagle International           知，金鷹國際提出的申索並無依據、申索
         is groundless, the likelihood that the claim will be decided in favour of      被判定金鷹國際勝訴的可能性極低，本集
         Golden Eagle International is very low, the risk exposure of the Group         團來自申索的風險極小且申索將不會對本
         from the claim is minimal and the claim will not have a material adverse       集團的業務及財務表現造成重大不利影
         impact on the Group’s operations and financial performance. The Group          響。本集團計劃在本公司的中國法律顧問
         plans to vehemently defend itself in the court with the assistance of the      的協助下於法庭進行有力抗辯。
